Public data for PPARγ, GCK, AURKA, and ERK2

Checked 18 November 2026. Key workbooks, spectra, curves, and the Lake supplement were opened directly. Large archive links come from repository manifests. PDB coverage uses PDBe UniProt mappings and RCSB metadata. The PDB index includes complexes and fragments.

Availability by target

TargetAvailable dataPublished results and gapsKey links
PPARγApo LBD HDX-MS, inverse-agonist HDX-MS, SEC-SAXS for full-length and truncated constructs, and NMR assignments for isolated AB and ligand-bound LBD.Published apo ¹⁹F exchange at 1.0 s⁻¹. Raw CEST series was not located.Apo HDX files · Inverse-agonist HDX files · Full-length SAXS .dat
GCKHuman apo and glucose-bound coordinates, NMR supplementary figures and tables, and separate variant activity and abundance scores.The primary CPMG fit is 509 ± 51 s⁻¹ and 16.5 ± 1.7% excited state at 313 K. Human solution SAXS and HDX are published. Public raw dispersion profiles, SAXS curves, and HDX runs were not located.1V4T apo mmCIF · 3IDH glucose-bound mmCIF · 2015 primary CPMG text
AURKAPublic single-molecule fluorescence and dwell-time archives, human kinase-domain SAXS, and raw and processed HDX-MS.Lake’s TR-FRET distances are in figures. The supplement contains numeric cooperativity factors for 24 inhibitors and the lifetime model. Per-well waveforms were not located.Lake supplement PDF · Single-molecule data · Dwell-time data · Unphosphorylated series
ERK2Rat apo 0P and 2P HDX uptake and representative raw spectra, plus apo and inhibitor NMR spectra and chemical-shift tables.Rat TEY sites are T183/Y185. Human sites are T185/Y187. The public inhibitor workbook excludes SCH772984. Xiao’s 2014 CPMG and free ERK2 SAXS curves were not located as downloadable numeric data.HDX uptake workbook · Apo NMR .ft2 · NMR peak table

PPARγChrisman et al. 2018NMR / CESTTwo resolved apo peaks exchange at 1.0 s⁻¹ (95% CI 0.9–1.1), 298.2 K.Rate comes from the engineered, fluorine-labelled C313A/Q322C-BTFA LBD reporter. See Supplementary Fig. 12c. Raw CEST series not located.Open source
